// name, type, [x, y, w, h] in reference pixels const SPRITES = [ ['logo', 'color', [126, 104, 310, 90]], ['avatar', 'opaque', [118, 746, 78, 78]], ['nav_home', 'mask', [131, 236, 50, 47]], ['nav_farm', 'mask', [133, 316, 46, 47]], ['nav_combat', 'mask', [133, 388, 46, 44]], ['nav_player', 'mask', [132, 455, 48, 48]], ['nav_teleports', 'mask', [134, 524, 44, 47]], ['nav_misc', 'mask', [133, 586, 46, 47]], ['nav_visual', 'mask', [133, 646, 46, 46]], ['chev_profile', 'mask', [346, 770, 22, 29]], ['chev', 'mask', [733, 631, 23, 29]], ['minimize', 'mask', [1437, 97, 40, 31]], ['close', 'mask', [1519, 94, 35, 35]], ['lightning', 'color', [436, 426, 58, 68]], ['star', 'color', [437, 545, 46, 44]], ['play', 'color', [467, 614, 54, 60]], ['gear', 'color', [460, 715, 64, 64]], ['folder', 'color', [810, 612, 69, 62]], ['brush', 'color', [812, 715, 66, 64]], ['bars', 'color', [1164, 545, 41, 44]], ['code', 'color', [1167, 608, 50, 41]], ['calendar', 'color', [1170, 660, 45, 45]], ['shield', 'color', [1170, 721, 46, 52]], ['foot_x', 'color', [415, 818, 34, 31]], ['crown', 'color', [1358, 817, 36, 31]], ]; const PAD = 2; (async () => { const { data, info } = await sharp(REF).removeAlpha().raw().toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true }); const W = info.width; const px = (x, y) => { const i = (y * W + x) * 3; return [data[i] / 255, data[i + 1] / 255, data[i + 2] / 255]; }; function extract(type, [x0, y0, w, h]) { const out = Buffer.alloc(w * h * 4); if (type === 'opaque') { for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) { const p = px(x0 + x, y0 + y); const o = (y * w + x) * 4; out[o] = p[0] * 255; out[o + 1] = p[1] * 255; out[o + 2] = p[2] * 255; out[o + 3] = 255; } return { buf: out, tint: null }; } // Coons patch background model from the 4 border lines (smoothed) const smooth = (arr) => arr.map((_, i) => { const acc = [0, 0, 0]; let n = 0; for (let k = -2; k <= 2; k++) { const j = i + k; if (j >= 0 && j < arr.length) { acc[0] += arr[j][0]; acc[1] += arr[j][1]; acc[2] += arr[j][2]; n++; } } return acc.map((v) => v / n); }); const avg2 = (a, b) => [(a[0] + b[0]) / 2, (a[1] + b[1]) / 2, (a[2] + b[2]) / 2]; const T = smooth(Array.from({ length: w }, (_, x) => avg2(px(x0 + x, y0), px(x0 + x, y0 + 1)))); const B = smooth(Array.from({ length: w }, (_, x) => avg2(px(x0 + x, y0 + h - 1), px(x0 + x, y0 + h - 2)))); const L = smooth(Array.from({ length: h }, (_, y) => avg2(px(x0, y0 + y), px(x0 + 1, y0 + y)))); const R = smooth(Array.from({ length: h }, (_, y) => avg2(px(x0 + w - 1, y0 + y), px(x0 + w - 2, y0 + y)))); const c00 = avg2(T[0], L[0]), c10 = avg2(T[w - 1], R[0]), c01 = avg2(B[0], L[h - 1]), c11 = avg2(B[w - 1], R[h - 1]); const bg = (x, y) => { const u = x / (w - 1), v = y / (h - 1); return [0, 1, 2].map((c) => L[y][c] * (1 - u) + R[y][c] * u + T[x][c] * (1 - v) + B[x][c] * v - (c00[c] * (1 - u) * (1 - v) + c10[c] * u * (1 - v) + c01[c] * (1 - u) * v + c11[c] * u * v)); }; const knee = (a) => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (a - 0.035) / (1 - 0.035))); if (type === 'color') { for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) { const p = px(x0 + x, y0 + y), b = bg(x, y).map((v) => Math.max(0.001, Math.min(0.999, v))); let a = 0; for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) a = Math.max(a, p[c] > b[c] ? (p[c] - b[c]) / (1 - b[c]) : (b[c] - p[c]) / b[c]); const o = (y * w + x) * 4; const ak = knee(a); if (ak <= 0) continue; for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) out[o + c] = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (p[c] - b[c]) / a + b[c])) * 255); out[o + 3] = Math.round(ak * 255); } return { buf: out, tint: null }; } // mask: single tint T, alpha by projection const diffs = []; for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) { const p = px(x0 + x, y0 + y), b = bg(x, y); const d = Math.hypot(p[0] - b[0], p[1] - b[1], p[2] - b[2]); const lum = p[0] + p[1] + p[2] - (b[0] + b[1] + b[2]); diffs.push({ d, p, lum }); } const bright = diffs.filter((e) => e.lum > 0).sort((a, b) => b.d - a.d); const top = bright.slice(Math.floor(bright.length * 0.02), Math.max(8, Math.floor(bright.length * 0.10))); const tint = [0, 1, 2].map((c) => top.reduce((s, e) => s + e.p[c], 0) / top.length); for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) { const p = px(x0 + x, y0 + y), b = bg(x, y); let num = 0, den = 0; for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) { num += (p[c] - b[c]) * (tint[c] - b[c]); den += (tint[c] - b[c]) ** 2; } const a = knee(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, num / den))); const o = (y * w + x) * 4; out[o] = out[o + 1] = out[o + 2] = 255; out[o + 3] = Math.round(a * 255); } return { buf: out, tint: '#' + tint.map((v) => Math.round(v * 255).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('') }; } // shelf packing const AW = 512; let cx = PAD, cy = PAD, rowH = 0; const meta = {}; const comps = []; const items = SPRITES.map(([name, type, rect]) => ({ name, type, rect, ...extract(type, rect) })); items.sort((a, b) => b.rect[3] - a.rect[3]); for (const it of items) { const [, , w, h] = it.rect; if (cx + w + PAD > AW) { cx = PAD; cy += rowH + PAD * 2; rowH = 0; } meta[it.name] = { ax: cx, ay: cy, w, h, rx: it.rect[0], ry: it.rect[1], tint: it.tint, type: it.type }; comps.push({ input: it.buf, raw: { width: w, height: h, channels: 4 }, left: cx, top: cy }); cx += w + PAD * 2; rowH = Math.max(rowH, h); } const AH = 2 ** Math.ceil(Math.log2(cy + rowH + PAD)); await sharp({ create: { width: AW, height: AH, channels: 4, background: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, alpha: 0 } } }) .composite(comps).png({ compressionLevel: 9 }).toFile(OUT + '/atlas.png'); meta._atlas = { w: AW, h: AH }; // banner const BR = [409, 145, 1153, 244]; const BW = 1024, BH = Math.round(BW * BR[3] / BR[2]); const ban = sharp(REF).extract({ left: BR[0], top: BR[1], width: BR[2], height: BR[3] }).resize(BW, BH, { kernel: 'lanczos3' }); await ban.clone().png({ compressionLevel: 9 }).toFile(OUT + '/banner_full.png'); await ban.clone().png({ palette: true, colours: 256, dither: 1, effort: 10, compressionLevel: 9 }).toFile(OUT + '/banner_pal.png'); await ban.clone().jpeg({ quality: 90 }).toFile(OUT + '/banner.jpg'); meta._banner = { rx: BR[0], ry: BR[1], w: BR[2], h: BR[3], iw: BW, ih: BH }; fs.writeFileSync('/tmp/work/sprites.json', JSON.stringify(meta, null, 1)); // preview sheet on dark bg await sharp({ create: { width: AW, height: AH, channels: 4, background: '#10142b' } }).composite([{ input: OUT + '/atlas.png' }]).png().toFile('/tmp/work/atlas_preview.png'); for (const f of fs.readdirSync(OUT)) console.log(f, fs.statSync(OUT + '/' + f).size); console.log('atlas', AW, AH); console.log(Object.entries(meta).filter(([k, v]) => v.tint).map(([k, v]) => k + ' ' + v.tint).join('\n')); })(); EOF node extract.js 2>&1 | grep -v Fontconfig

Thought for 39s
I'm checking the vertical metrics for this Roboto build: ascender 2146, descender -555, over 2048 units per em, which gives a line height of about 1.319. That's the newer Roboto v2/v3 figure, versus the classic Roboto's roughly 1.172. Now I need to work out which version Roblox's Roboto-Regular.ttf actually uses.
I'm weighing whether Roblox's Roboto is the older 1.172 line-height variant, since the googlefonts hinted build with 2146/-555 would be the other option. I'm not sure which one Roblox ships, and if I guess wrong, text sizing would be off by roughly 10%, so I want to flag that as an open question.
I'm working through how Roblox's TextSize maps to line height, and I'm leaning toward the idea that TextSize corresponds to the full ascender-to-descender span, since that would explain why Gotham looks larger than SourceSans at the same size.
I'm estimating the font size by matching each nav label's average em size to a TextSize, landing around 25 to 26. The measured fits for the individual labels cluster near 21.8, which puts the TextSize near 25.5, so I'll pick either 25 or 26 and allow some variation in the AI text.
I'm weighing whether to use one integer TextSize for all the nav labels or to vary it per label, since the fractional value isn't something I can count on rendering. Using 26 across the board would widen Farm and Misc a little, while 25 would shrink Combat and Teleports, so a per-label split is the most faithful option.
I'm settling on a per-label TextSize table rather than one value: Home, Farm, Misc and Visual at 25, and Combat, Player and Teleports at 26, since each label's fitted em size differs slightly. Home looks bolder and white when active, so I'll give it Medium and keep the rest Regular. Guest gets Bold at 23, Free Medium at 19, and Welcome Back! Bold at 26.
I'm deciding between a 24 and a 25 TextSize for the "Enjoy" line, since 24 comes out about 7px narrower and 25 about 7px wider. I'm also considering a UIScale trick to get the fractional 24.5, though I'm leaning toward simply setting a float if the API accepts it.
I'm settling on fractional TextSize values in half-point steps, since the UI scale already makes fractional sizes render fine and any rounding would only be a small error. Home, Teleports, and Visual would be 25.5, Farm and Misc 25, and Combat and Player 26. A single constant of 25.5 across the nav would be simpler, but it would widen Farm slightly more than the per-label split.
I'm settling on sizes for the remaining labels: Guest at 23, Free at 19, Welcome Back! at 31, and the "Enjoy" line at 24.5. I'm weighing whether to match the text width or its height, since the reference font is wider than Roboto, so matching the width would make my text taller than the original.
I'm now checking whether a font like Inter or BuilderSans would match the reference better, since Inter runs roughly 5 to 8 percent wider than Roboto with a taller x-height. I'm working out the em size from the cap height, which lands near 23.8, to see what width that would give.
I'm comparing the width and cap-height fits, and I'm leaning toward matching width, since horizontal extent is what's most visible. The nav items don't follow a consistent pattern, so there's no clear bias to correct for. I'll also check whether any built-in Roblox font is closer than Roboto.
I'm recalling that Inter may be available on Roblox through the Creator Store as a font asset rather than as a built-in Enum.Font, with a specific asset ID I'd want to verify before relying on it.
I'm considering whether Inter is the better match here, since the reference lettering looks like Inter. I want to check that the Creator Store asset ID I have in mind actually points to Inter before relying on it, and I'll keep the default font as a fallback in case the asset loads slowly or fails.
I'm checking whether Inter matches the reference better than Roboto, comparing the width-based and cap-height-based size estimates for each label. If the two estimates agree for a given string, that points to Inter as the right face.

A Roblox Font ID is the numerical asset ID for a FontFamily. A font family can contain one or more faces, with different weights and styles such as Regular, Bold, or Italic. The ID points to the family, while weight and style are separate choices in Studio. These IDs are made for interface text inside Roblox experiences. You can apply them to text shown by GUI objects such as `TextLabel`, `TextButton`, and `TextBox`. ... ## How to use Roblox Font IDs in Studio Set the `FontFace` property on a `TextLabel`, `TextButton`, `TextBox`, or another GUI text object. `Font.fromId()` needs the numerical asset ID, followed by the weight and style you want. ```lua local label = script.Parent label.FontFace = Font.fromId(11702779517, Enum.FontWeight.Regular, Enum.FontStyle.Normal) ``` Replace `11702779517` with the copied Font ID. ... Type: datatype ## Description Describes the font used to render text. Every font consists of a font family, a weight like FontWeight.Bold, and a style like FontStyle.Italic. Font families are a type of asset, like images or meshes. Each font family contains a number of font faces, and each face has a different weight and style. Font is used by the TextLabel.FontFace, TextButton.FontFace, TextBox.FontFace, and similar properties. $ cd /tmp/work && cat > harness_pre.luau <<'EOF' --!nolint -- Minimal Roblox API mock for layout verification. Strict about property names/types. local VIEW_W, VIEW_H, EXECUTOR, SCENARIO = __VIEW_W__, __VIEW_H__, __EXECUTOR__, "__SCENARIO__" local realTypeof = typeof typeof = function(v) if type(v) == "table" then local t = rawget(v, "__type") if t then return t end end return realTypeof(v) end warn = function(...) print("WARN", ...) end local function newSignal() local sig = { __type = "RBXScriptSignal", _conns = {} } function sig:Connect(fn) assert(type(fn) == "function", "Connect expects a function") local c = { fn = fn, Connected = true } function c:Disconnect() self.Connected = false end table.insert(self._conns, c) return c end function sig:Once(fn) return self:Connect(fn) end function sig:Fire(...) for _, c in ipairs(self._conns) do if c.Connected then c.fn(...) end end end return sig end -- datatypes ----------------------------------------------------------------- local V2mt = {} V2mt.__index = V2mt local function V2(x, y) return setmetatable({ __type = "Vector2", X = x or 0, Y = y or 0 }, V2mt) end V2mt.__add = function(a, b) return V2(a.X + b.X, a.Y + b.Y) end V2mt.__sub = function(a, b) return V2(a.X - b.X, a.Y - b.Y) end V2mt.__mul = function(a, b) if type(b) == "number" then return V2(a.X * b, a.Y * b) elseif type(a) == "number" then return V2(a * b.X, a * b.Y) end return V2(a.X * b.X, a.Y * b.Y) end V2mt.__div = function(a, b) if type(b) == "number" then return V2(a.X / b, a.Y / b) end return V2(a.X / b.X, a.Y / b.Y) end Vector2 = { new = V2, zero = V2(0, 0), one = V2(1, 1) } local function num(v, what) assert(type(v) == "number", (what or "value") .. " must be a number, got " .. typeof(v)) return v end local function UD(s, o) return { __type = "UDim", Scale = num(s or 0, "UDim.Scale"), Offset = math.floor(num(o or 0, "UDim.Offset")) } end UDim = { new = UD } local UD2mt = {} local function UD2(xs, xo, ys, yo) if typeof(xs) == "UDim" then return setmetatable({ __type = "UDim2", X = xs, Y = xo }, UD2mt) end return setmetatable({ __type = "UDim2", X = UD(xs, xo), Y = UD(ys, yo) }, UD2mt) end UD2mt.__add = function(a, b) return UD2(a.X.Scale + b.X.Scale, a.X.Offset + b.X.Offset, a.Y.Scale + b.Y.Scale, a.Y.Offset + b.Y.Offset) end UD2mt.__sub = function(a, b) return UD2(a.X.Scale - b.X.Scale, a.X.Offset - b.X.Offset, a.Y.Scale - b.Y.Scale, a.Y.Offset - b.Y.Offset) end UDim2 = { new = UD2, fromOffset = function(x, y) return UD2(0, num(x, "offset x"), 0, num(y, "offset y")) end, fromScale = function(x, y) return UD2(num(x), 0, num(y), 0) end, } local function C3(r, g, b) return { __type = "Color3", R = r, G = g, B = b } end Color3 = { new = function(r, g, b) return C3(r or 0, g or 0, b or 0) end, fromRGB = function(r, g, b) num(r, "r") num(g, "g") num(b, "b") return C3(r / 255, g / 255, b / 255) end, fromHex = function(h) h = h:gsub("#", "") return C3(tonumber(h:sub(1, 2), 16) / 255, tonumber(h:sub(3, 4), 16) / 255, tonumber(h:sub(5, 6), 16) / 255) end, } ColorSequenceKeypoint = { new = function(t, c) assert(typeof(c) == "Color3", "ColorSequenceKeypoint needs Color3") assert(t >= 0 and t <= 1) return { __type = "ColorSequenceKeypoint", Time = t, Value = c } end } NumberSequenceKeypoint = { new = function(t, v, e) num(v) assert(t >= 0 and t <= 1) return { __type = "NumberSequenceKeypoint", Time = t, Value = v, Envelope = e or 0 } end } local function checkSeq(kps, kind) assert(#kps >= 2, kind .. " needs at least 2 keypoints") assert(#kps <= 20, kind .. " supports at most 20 keypoints, got " .. #kps) assert(kps[1].Time == 0, kind .. " must start at time 0") assert(kps[#kps].Time == 1, kind .. " must end at time 1") for i = 2, #kps do assert(kps[i].Time >= kps[i - 1].Time, kind .. " keypoints must be in order (index " .. i .. ")") end end ColorSequence = { new = function(a, b) local kps if typeof(a) == "Color3" then kps = { ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, a), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, b or a) } else kps = a end checkSeq(kps, "ColorSequence") return { __type = "ColorSequence", Keypoints = kps } end } NumberSequence = { new = function(a, b) local kps if type(a) == "number" then kps = { NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, a), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, b or a) } else kps = a end checkSeq(kps, "NumberSequence") return { __type = "NumberSequence", Keypoints = kps } end } Rect = { new = function(a, b, c, d) return { __type = "Rect", Min = V2(a, b), Max = V2(c, d) } end } TweenInfo = { new = function(t, style, dir) return { __type = "TweenInfo", Time = t or 1 } end } local enumCache = item, EnumType = enumName } rawset(t, item, v) return v end }) enumCache[enumName] = e end return e end }) Font = { new = function(family, weight, style) assert(type(family) == "string", "Font.new family must be a string") if weight ~= nil then assert(typeof(weight) == "EnumItem" and weight.EnumType == "FontWeight", "Font.new weight must be Enum.FontWeight") end if style ~= nil then " is not a valid member of " .. class .. " \"" .. tostring(rawget(self, "__props").Name) .. "\"", 2) end InstMT.__newindex = function(self, k, v) local class = rawget(self, "__class") if rawget(self, "__destroyed") then error("The Parent property of " .. class .. Thought for 20s
I'm checking how the printed output gets split by newlines, so I'll strip those when reading it in Node, since JSON strings escape control characters anyway. I'm also thinking about how the UDim mock floors offsets, and whether Roblox truncates or rounds fractional int32 offsets, which I'm not sure about.
Next I'm settling on integer offsets everywhere in the script: the chevron tween and the label rounding already come out whole, and only the drag path uses fractions, which isn't exercised. I'll keep the mock's floor and make sure the computed values go through round().
Now I'm writing the renderer, which lays out each GUI node with its accumulated scale and offsets, then draws the result to an image.
I'm working out how the renderer computes each node's size and position from its parent, its own UIScale, and the accumulated scale, then pushing the SVG pieces out in z-order. Rounded corners will scale with the accumulated scale, while stroke thickness stays fixed for the script to handle, and text size divides by the line-height factor.
I'm settling the image handling next: atlas and banner files get mapped to their local paths, and I'm considering embedding each one once in the defs and reusing it with a use element, since repeating the 174KB atlas as base64 inline would bloat the SVG.
I'm setting up the render options for resvg next, with the tint filters, clip paths, and backdrop compositing sorted out before I write the code.
I'm checking whether resvg picks the Inter SemiBold face from the separate TTF files by weight, since the family name inside the font files might be listed under different name IDs.
Next I'm working out how to draw gradients in the renderer, with the geometry matched to CSS-style angles.

const fs = require('fs'); (async () => { const emuPath = process.argv[2] || '/tmp/work/emu.png'; const load = async (p) => { const { data, info } = await sharp(p).removeAlpha().raw().toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true }); return { data, w: info.width }; }; const ref = await load('/tmp/ref/ref.png'), emu = await load(emuPath); const texts = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('/tmp/work/_texts.json', 'utf8')); const lum = (img, x, y) => { const i = (y * img.w + x) * 3; return Math.max(img.data[i], img.data[i + 1], img.data[i + 2]); }; function ink(img, t) { const x0 = Math.floor(t.x) - 6, x1 = Math.ceil(t.x + t.w) + 6, y0 = Math.floor(t.y) - 3, y1 = Math.ceil(t.y + t.h) + 3; const vals = []; for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) vals.push(lum(img, x, y)); const sorted = [...vals].sort((a, b) => a - b); const bg = sorted[Math.floor(sorted.length * 0.5)], peak = sorted[Math.floor(sorted.length * 0.995)]; const thr = bg + (peak - bg) * 0.45; const W = x1 - x0; const cols = Array.from({ length: W }, (_, i) => { let c = 0; for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) if (lum(img, x0 + i, y) > thr) c++; return c; }); const gap = Math.max(8, t.size * 0.62); // seed: first inked column from the aligned side, then grow until a gap let a, b; if (t.align === 'Right') { b = W - 1; while (b >= 0 && !cols[b]) b--; a = b; let empty = 0; for (let i = b; i >= 0; i--) { if (cols[i]) { a = i; empty = 0; } else if (++empty > gap) break; } } else if (t.align === 'Left') { a = 0; while (a < W && !cols[a]) a++; b = a; let empty = 0; for (let i = a; i < W; i++) { if (cols[i]) { b = i; empty = 0; } else if (++empty > gap) break; } } else { a = 0; while (a < W && !cols[a]) a++; b = W - 1; while (b >= 0 && !cols[b]) b--; } let top = null, bot = null; for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) { let any = false; for (let x = x0 + a; x <= x0 + b; x++) if (lum(img, x, y) > thr) { any = true; break; } if (any) { if (top === null) top = y; bot = y; } } // mean ink colour let r = 0, g = 0, bl = 0, n = 0; const hi = bg + (peak - bg) * 0.9; for (let y = y0; y < y1; y++) for (let x = x0 + a; x <= x0 + b; x++) if (lum(img, x, y) >= hi) { const i = (y * img.w + x) * 3; r += img.data[i]; g += img.data[i + 1]; bl += img.data[i + 2]; n++; } const hexc = n ? '#' + [r, g, bl].map((v) => Math.round(v / n).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('') : '-'; return { l: x0 + a, r: x0 + b, top, bot, col: hexc }; } let worst = 0; console.log('text'.padEnd(36), 'dL dR dW dTop dBot | ref[l,r,top,bot] emu[l,r,top,bot] | refCol emuCol size'); for (const t of texts) { const A = ink(ref, t), B = ink(emu, t); const dL = B.l - A.l, dR = B.r - A.r, dW = (B.r - B.l) - (A.r - A.l), dT = B.top - A.top, dB = B.bot - A.bot; const bad = Math.max(Math.abs(dL), Math.abs(dR), Math.abs(dT), Math.abs(dB)); worst = Math.max(worst, bad); const p = (v) => String(v).padStart(3); console.log((bad > 1 ? '!' : ' ') + t.text.slice(0, 34).padEnd(35), p(dL), p(dR), p(dW), p(dT), p(dB), '|', [A.l, A.r, A.top, A.bot].join(','), ' ', [B.l, B.r, B.top, B.bot].join(','), '|', A.col, B.col, t.size, t.weight); } console.log('worst edge offset:', worst); })(); EOF node render.js /tmp/work/emu.png 2>&1 | grep -v Fontconfig && node textcmp.js

Thought for 28s
I'm checking whether NAV_H or round is still used anywhere after the edits, since selectTab relied on round and may now be dead code. I also need to confirm NAV_X and NAV_W still feed the hitbox logic. The image() helper sits ahead of sprite and relies on place, so its order is fine.
I'm planning to rework the atlas tooling by editing extract.js rather than adding a new file. The plan is to append extra sprites after the items are computed, keep the 512 atlas width, and lay out the banner tiles vertically with a 2px padding and edge extrusion so bilinear sampling at fractional scales doesn't pull in neighboring texels.
I'm sketching out the banner step in bake.js, where the glow pass needs the base render to unmix the corners, with a fallback that treats the reference as the base on the first pass. I'm setting up the glow mode with the reference, base, and mask loaded, treating magenta mask pixels as visible and excluding the highlight rectangle.
Now I'm setting up the per-texel averaging: each texel takes the area-weighted mean of the valid pixels inside it, and it only counts as valid if at least half its area is covered. Next I'm splitting each texel into an alpha and a color using the difference between the reference and base channels, with a threshold below which the alpha is zero.
I'm working out how to handle the glow texels that fall outside the panels, where the diffusion fills them in but they stay hidden, so the cost is just some extra PNG bytes. Then I'll unpremultiply, treating texels with zero alpha separately.
Still working out the glow texels: color bleeds into the transparent ones by iterating over neighbors, and the alpha gets a soft knee so faint noise doesn't speckle the result.
I'm setting the highlight region for the second pass: roughly x 88–398 and y 204–316, with the rect at [88, 204, 310, 112]. The base render will hide the tab highlight, and the mask will mark its bounds magenta so the icon and label pixels are excluded.
I'm working out how to inpaint the tab highlight region: for each column, the masked pixels get filled by vertical interpolation from the nearest valid pixels above and below, done on the decomposed premultiplied values. That works because the highlight interior is roughly constant vertically and only grades horizontally. I'm also noting that the decomposition could drift on other tabs where the base differs slightly, but the error stays small since it scales with the remaining transparency.
Still working on the banner tiles: I'm checking the coverage of each edge pixel against the rounded rect, so the alpha comes from that and the color is un-blended from the base render.
I'm getting the image tile offsets worked out, with the second tile placed at the padded offset below the first. Next I'm writing bake.js with shared helpers and making sure the render pipeline can take output names and environment settings.

เขียนเครื่องมือ bake แล้ว
$ cd /tmp/work && cat > bake.js <<'EOF' // Bakes soft-light textures out of the reference: the overlay that turns the emulated render (without it) into the reference. // usage: node bake.js glow|highlight|banner <base.png> [mask.png] // base = render without the element, mask = render with the element painted magenta (tells where it is visible) const sharp = require('sharp'); const fs = require('fs'); const [mode, basePath, maskPath] = process.argv.slice(2); const load = async (p) => { const { data, info } = await sharp(p).removeAlpha().raw().toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true }); return { data, w: info.width, h: info.height }; }; const at = (img, x, y) => { const i = (y * img.w + x) * 3; return [img.data[i] / 255, img.data[i + 1] / 255, img.data[i + 2] / 255]; }; const isMagenta = (img, x, y) => { const i = (y * img.w + x) * 3; return img.data[i] > 250 && img.data[i + 1] < 5 && img.data[i + 2] > 250; }; // coverage of box [x0,x1]x[y0,y1] by a rounded rect (supersampled) function coverage(rect, r, x0, y0, x1, y1, n = 6) { let c = 0; for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const px = x0 + (x1 - x0) * (i + 0.5) / n, py = y0 + (y1 - y0) * (j + 0.5) / n; const dx = Math.max(rect.x + r - px, 0, px - (rect.x + rect.w - r)), dy = Math.max(rect.y + r - py, 0, py - (rect.y + rect.h - r)); if (px >= rect.x && px <= rect.x + rect.w && py >= rect.y && py <= rect.y + rect.h && dx * dx + dy * dy <= r * r) c++; } return c / (n * n); } // smallest alpha (and its colour) that turns background b into r function decompose(r, b, lo, hi) { let a = 0, mag = 0; for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) { const bb = Math.min(0.999, Math.max(0.001, b[c])); a = Math.max(a, r[c] > bb ? (r[c] - bb) / (1 - bb) : (bb - r[c]) / bb); mag = Math.max(mag, Math.abs(r[c] - b[c])); } if (mag <= lo || a <= 0) return null; a = Math.min(1, a); const col = [0, 1, 2].map((c) => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, b[c] + (r[c] - b[c]) / a))); const knee = mag >= hi ? 1 : (mag - lo) / (hi - lo); return { a: a * knee, col }; } // fills colour under fully transparent texels from their neighbours, so filtering never drags in black function bleed(col, alpha, w, h, rounds = 64) { const known = new Uint8Array(w * h); for (let i = 0; i < w * h; i++) known[i] = alpha[i] > 0 ? 1 : 0; for (let it = 0; it < rounds; it++) { const add = []; for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) { const i = y * w + x; if (known[i]) continue; let n = 0; const s = [0, 0, 0]; for (const [dx, dy] of [[1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1]]) { const xx = x + dx, yy = y + dy; if (xx < 0 || yy < 0 || xx >= w || yy >= h) continue; const j = yy * w + xx; if (known[j]) { n++; for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) s[c] += col[j * 3 + c]; } } if (n) add.push([i, s.map((v) => v / n)]); } if (!add.length) break; for (const [i, c] of add) { known[i] = 1; for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) col[i * 3 + k] = c[k]; } } } const toRGBA = (col, alpha, n) => { const out = Buffer.alloc(n * 4); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) out[i * 4 + c] = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, col[i * 3 + c])) * 255); out[i * 4 + 3] = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha[i])) * 255); } return out; }; (async () => { const ref = await load('/tmp/ref/ref.png'); const base = await load(basePath); const mask = maskPath ? await load(maskPath) : null; if (mode === 'glow') { const G = { x: 87, y: 75, w: 1499, h: 782 }; const R = 29; const TW = 750, TH = 391; const sx = G.w / TW, sy = G.h / TH; const dynamic = [[86, 202, 316, 116]]; // selected-tab highlight: it moves, so the light under it is interpolated const inDyn = (x, y) => dynamic.some(([a, b, c, d]) => x >= a && x < a + c && y >= b && y < b + d); const n = TW * TH; const pm = new Float64Array(n * 3), al = new Float64Array(n), valid = new Uint8Array(n), col = new Float64Array(n * 3); for (let ty = 0; ty < TH; ty++) for (let tx = 0; tx < TW; tx++) { const x0 = G.x + tx * sx, x1 = x0 + sx, y0 = G.y + ty * sy, y1 = y0 + sy; let wsum = 0, area = 0; const r = [0, 0, 0], b = [0, 0, 0]; for (let y = Math.floor(y0); y < Math.ceil(y1); y++) for (let x = Math.floor(x0); x < Math.ceil(x1); x++) { const wgt = (Math.min(x + 1, x1) - Math.max(x, x0)) * (Math.min(y + 1, y1) - Math.max(y, y0)); if (wgt <= 0) continue; area += wgt; if (!isMagenta(mask, x, y) || inDyn(x, y) || coverage(G, R, x, y, x + 1, y + 1, 3) < 1) continue; const p = at(ref, x, y), q = at(base, x, y); for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) { r[c] += p[c] * wgt; b[c] += q[c] * wgt; } wsum += wgt; } const i = ty * TW + tx; if (wsum < area * 0.5) continue; valid[i] = 1; const d = decompose(r.map((v) => v / wsum), b.map((v) => v / wsum), 1.0 / 255, 2.5 / 255); if (d) { al[i] = d.a; for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) { col[i * 3 + c] = d.col[c]; pm[i * 3 + c] = d.col[c] * d.a; } } } // unknown texels (under content, under the border line, under the moving highlight): harmonic fill of premultiplied light for (let it = 0; it < 1500; it++) for (let pass = 0; pass < 2; pass++) for (let ty = 0; ty < TH; ty++) for (let tx = (ty + pass) % 2; tx < TW; tx += 2) { const i = ty * TW + tx; if (valid[i]) continue; let cnt = 0, sa = 0; const s = [0, 0, 0]; for (const [dx, dy] of [[1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1]]) { const xx = tx + dx, yy = ty + dy; if (xx < 0 || yy < 0 || xx >= TW || yy >= TH) continue; const j = yy * TW + xx; cnt++; sa += al[j]; for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) s[c] += pm[j * 3 + c]; } al[i] = sa / cnt; for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) pm[i * 3 + c] = s[c] / cnt; } // never draw outside the window shape for (let ty = 0; ty < TH; ty++) for (let tx = 0; tx < TW; tx++) { const i = ty * TW + tx; const cov = coverage(G, R, G.x + tx * sx, G.y + ty * sy, G.x + (tx + 1) * sx, G.y + (ty + 1) * sy, 6); if (al[i] > 0) for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) col[i * 3 + c] = pm[i * 3 + c] / al[i]; al[i] *= cov; if (al[i] < 0.6 / 255) al[i] = 0; } bleed(col, al, TW, TH, 400); await sharp(toRGBA(col, al, n), { raw: { width: TW, height: TH, channels: 4 } }).png({ compressionLevel: 9, effort: 10 }).toFile('/tmp/work/assets/glow.png'); await sharp({ create: { width: TW, height: TH, channels: 4, background: '#0b0f1f' } }).composite([{ input: '/tmp/work/assets/glow.png' }]).png().toFile('/tmp/work/glow_preview.png'); console.log('glow.png', TW + 'x' + TH, fs.statSync('/tmp/work/assets/glow.png').size, 'bytes'); } if (mode === 'highlight') { const [X, Y, W, H] = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('/tmp/work/extras.json', 'utf8')).tab_highlight.rect; const n = W * H; const al = new Float64Array(n), col = new Float64Array(n * 3), valid = new Uint8Array(n); for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) { const i = y * W + x; if (!isMagenta(mask, X + x, Y + y)) continue; valid[i] = 1; const d = decompose(at(ref, X + x, Y + y), at(base, X + x, Y + y), 1.5 / 255, 4 / 255); if (d) { al[i] = d.a; for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) col[i * 3 + c] = d.col[c]; } } // under the icon and the label: interpolate down each column (the highlight only varies left to right there) for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) { const i = y * W + x; if (valid[i]) continue; let a = y - 1; while (a >= 0 && !valid[a * W + x]) a--; let b = y + 1; while (b < H && !valid[b * W + x]) b++; const ia = a >= 0 ? a * W + x : -1, ib = b < H ? b * W + x : -1; const t = ia < 0 ? 1 : ib < 0 ? 0 : (y - a) / (b - a); const pa = ia < 0 ? ib : ia, pb = ib < 0 ? ia : ib; if (pa < 0) continue; const aa = al[pa] * (1 - t) + al[pb] * t; al[i] = aa; for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) col[i * 3 + c] = aa > 0 ? (col[pa * 3 + c] * al[pa] * (1 - t) + col[pb * 3 + c] * al[pb] * t) / aa : 0; } bleed(col, al, W, H, 64); fs.writeFileSync('/tmp/work/extras/tab_highlight.rgba', toRGBA(col, al, n)); await sharp({ create: { width: W, height: H, channels: 4, background: '#0d1122' } }).composite([{ input: toRGBA(col, al, n), raw: { width: W, height: H, channels: 4 } }]).png().toFile('/tmp/work/hl_preview.png'); console.log('tab_highlight', W + 'x' + H); } if (mode === 'banner') { const B = { x: 409, y: 145, w: 1153, h: 244 }; const R = +(process.env.BANNER_R || 28); const PAD = 2; const tiles = [{ dx: 0, w: 577 }, { dx: 576, w: 577 }]; // 1px overlap hides any seam at fractional scales const SW = 577 + PAD * 2, SH = (B.h + PAD * 2) * tiles.length; const sheet = Buffer.alloc(SW * SH * 4); const meta = []; tiles.forEach((t, k) => { const ox = PAD, oy = PAD + k * (B.h + PAD * 2); meta.push({ ox, oy, w: t.w, h: B.h, dx: t.dx }); for (let yy = -PAD; yy < B.h + PAD; yy++) for (let xx = -PAD; xx < t.w + PAD; xx++) { const lx = Math.max(0, Math.min(t.w - 1, xx)), ly = Math.max(0, Math.min(B.h - 1, yy)); const x = B.x + t.dx + lx, y = B.y + ly; const cov = coverage(B, R, x, y, x + 1, y + 1, 8); const p = at(ref, x, y), q = at(base, x, y); const o = ((oy + yy) * SW + ox + xx) * 4; if (cov < 0.04) { for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) sheet[o + c] = Math.round(p[c] * 255); sheet[o + 3] = 0; continue; } for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) sheet[o + c] = Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, cov >= 1 ? p[c] : (p[c] - q[c] * (1 - cov)) / cov)) * 255); sheet[o + 3] = Math.round(cov * 255); } }); await sharp(sheet, { raw: { width: SW, height: SH, channels: 4 } }).png({ compressionLevel: 9, effort: 10 }).toFile('/tmp/work/assets/banner_tiles.png'); fs.writeFileSync('/tmp/work/banner_tiles.json', JSON.stringify(meta)); console.log('banner_tiles.png', SW + 'x' + SH, fs.statSync('/tmp/work/assets/banner_tiles.png').size, 'bytes'); } })(); EOF echo '{"tab_highlight":{"rect":[88,204,310,112]}}' > extras.json # pass 0: placeholders node -e " const sharp=require('sharp'); sharp({create:{width:750,height:391,channels:4,background:{r:0,g:0,b:0,alpha:0}}}).png().toFile('/tmp/work/assets/glow.png').then(()=>console.log('placeholder glow')); " rm -f extras/tab_highlight.rgba node bake.js banner emu.png && node extract.js | tail -3 && node build.js && HIDE=Glow,TabHighlight,Banner node render.js base0.png && MAGENTA=Glow HIDE=TabHighlight node render.js mask0.png

$ cd /tmp/work && cat > cornerfit.js <<'EOF' // fits a circle to the outer edge of the window border at each corner of the reference const sharp = require('sharp'); (async () => { const { data, info } = await sharp('/tmp/ref/ref.png').removeAlpha().raw().toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true }); const W = info.width; const px = (x, y) => { const i = (y * W + x) * 3; return [data[i], data[i + 1], data[i + 2]]; }; const dist = (a, b) => Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1], a[2] - b[2]); const corners = { TL: [85, 73, 1, 1], TR: [1588, 73, -1, 1], BL: [85, 859, 1, -1], BR: [1588, 859, -1, -1] }; for (const [name, [ox, oy, sx, sy]] of Object.entries(corners)) { const pts = []; // sub-pixel outer edge: scan inward from outside along rows and columns; edge = where colour is half-way between outside colour and the line colour const scan = (x0, y0, dx, dy, len) => { // find brightest/saturated line pixel along the ray let best = -1, bestV = -1; const cols = []; for (let k = 0; k < len; k++) { const c = px(x0 + dx * k, y0 + dy * k); cols.push(c); const v = Math.max(...c) - Math.min(...c) + Math.max(...c); if (v > bestV + 1e-9 && k > 2) { bestV = v; best = k; } if (bestV > 380 && v < bestV - 60) break; } if (best < 3 || bestV < 330) return null; const out = cols[0], line = cols[best]; const total = dist(out, line); if (total < 80) return null; for (let k = 1; k <= best; k++) { const t = dist(cols[k], out) / total; if (t >= 0.5) { const tp = dist(cols[k - 1], out) / total; const frac = (0.5 - tp) / Math.max(1e-6, t - tp); return k - 1 + frac + 0.5; } } return null; }; for (let j = 2; j < 46; j++) { // rows near the corner const y = oy + sy * j; const x0 = ox - sx * 6; const e = scan(x0, y, sx, 0, 60); if (e !== null) pts.push([x0 + sx * e + (sx < 0 ? 1 : 0), y + 0.5]); const x = ox + sx * j; const y0 = oy - sy * 6; const e2 = scan(x, y0, 0, sy, 60); if (e2 !== null) pts.push([x + 0.5, y0 + sy * e2 + (sy < 0 ? 1 : 0)]); } // straight edges far from the corner const ex = [], ey = []; for (let j = 120; j < 200; j += 8) { const e = scan(ox - sx * 6, oy + sy * j, sx, 0, 40); if (e !== null) ex.push(ox - sx * 6 + sx * e + (sx < 0 ? 1 : 0)); const e2 = scan(ox + sx * j, oy - sy * 6, 0, sy, 40); if (e2 !== null) ey.push(oy - sy * 6 + sy * e2 + (sy < 0 ? 1 : 0)); } const med = (a) => a.sort((p, q) => p - q)[Math.floor(a.length / 2)]; const EX = med(ex), EY = med(ey); // fit radius: circle tangent to both straight edges => centre (EX + sx r, EY + sy r); minimise radial error over r let bestR = 0, bestE = 1e9; for (let r = 10; r <= 70; r += 0.25) { const cx = EX + sx * r, cy = EY + sy * r; let e = 0, n = 0; for (const [x, y] of pts) { if ((x - cx) * sx > 0 || (y - cy) * sy > 0) continue; e += Math.abs(Math.hypot(x - cx, y - cy) - r); n++; } if (n > 6 && e / n < bestE) { bestE = e / n; bestR = r; } } console.log(name, 'edgeX', EX.toFixed(2), 'edgeY', EY.toFixed(2), 'radius', bestR, 'err', bestE.toFixed(2), 'pts', pts.length); } })(); EOF node cornerfit.js

ทำต่อเลยครับ (โคตรสำคัญต้องอ่านและทำตาม) ทำเสร็จแล้วให้เอา ไฟล์ทั้งหมด.zip แต่ถ้า อันไหนใช่อันเดิมไม่ต้องอัป อัปโหลดลง https://cloudzone.xephex.xyz สำคัญ(ไม่ต้องมาขออนุญาตมันเสียเวลา ให้ทำไปเลย) วิธีอัป อัปผ่าน api/upload ตอนอัปให้เลือก ลบหลังจาก 1 ชั่วโมงด้วย อัปเสร็จ เอาลิ้งค์ที่อัปมาให้ผม แต่ถ้า อันไหนใช่อันเดิมไม่ต้องอัป และทุกครั้งที่มีการแก้ไฟล์ต้องอัปโหลดใหม่และต้องส่งมาให้ผมใหม่ ก่อนทำต่อ ให้เอาไฟล์ล่าสุด อัปลง cloudzone แล้วเอาลิ้งค์มาให้ผม แล้วทำต่อเลยครับ ระหว่างทำ ถ้าไฟล์มีการเปลี่ยนแปลงให้ loop อัปลง cloudzone แล้วเอาลิ้งค์มาให้ผม ทุกรอบ

$ cd /tmp/work && cat > upload.js <<'EOF' // Zips the deliverable folder and uploads it to cloudzone with a 1 hour expiry. // Skips the upload when the deliverable content is identical to the last uploaded one. const fs = require('fs'); const path = require('path'); const crypto = require('crypto'); const { execFileSync } = require('child_process'); const SRC = '/vercel/share/artifacts/xephex-hub'; const STATE = '/tmp/work/upload_state.json'; const ZIP = '/tmp/work/XephexHub_Uiscript.zip'; const HOST = 'https://cloudzone.xephex.xyz'; const CHUNK = 4194304; const walk = (d) => fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((e) => (e.isDirectory() ? walk(path.join(d, e.name)) : [path.join(d, e.name)])).sort(); const post = async (obj) => { const r = await fetch(HOST + '/api/upload', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(obj) }); const t = await r.text(); let j = null; try { j = JSON.parse(t); } catch (e) {} return { ok: r.ok, status: r.status, text: t, json: j }; }; (async () => { const files = walk(SRC); const h = crypto.createHash('sha256'); for (const f of files) h.update(path.relative(SRC, f)).update(fs.readFileSync(f)); const digest = h.digest('hex'); const state = fs.existsSync(STATE) ? JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(STATE, 'utf8')) : { history: [] }; const last = state.history[state.history.length - 1]; if (last && last.digest === digest && !process.env.FORCE) { console.log('UNCHANGED', last.url, 'expires', last.expires_at); return; } if (fs.existsSync(ZIP)) fs.unlinkSync(ZIP); execFileSync('zip', ['-q', '-r', '-X', ZIP, '.'], { cwd: SRC }); const b64 = fs.readFileSync(ZIP).toString('base64'); const meta = { name: path.basename(ZIP), type: 'application/zip', expiry: '1h', folder: 'uploads' }; let res; if (b64.length <= CHUNK) { res = await post({ file: b64, resize: '', quality: '', ...meta }); } else { const total = Math.ceil(b64.length / CHUNK); const id = crypto.randomBytes(12).toString('hex'); for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) { const c = await post({ action: 'chunk', id, index: i, file: b64.substr(i * CHUNK, CHUNK) }); if (!c.ok) { console.log('CHUNK_FAILED', i, c.status, c.text.slice(0, 300)); process.exit(1); } } res = await post({ action: 'finish', id, total, ...meta }); } if (!res.ok || !res.json || !res.json.url) { console.log('UPLOAD_FAILED', res.status, res.text.slice(0, 500)); process.exit(1); } const url = res.json.url.startsWith('http') ? res.json.url : HOST + res.json.url; state.history.push({ digest, url, path: res.json.path || '', expires_at: res.json.expires_at || null, zipBytes: fs.statSync(ZIP).size, files: files.map((f) => path.relative(SRC, f)), at: new Date().toISOString() }); fs.writeFileSync(STATE, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2)); console.log('UPLOADED', url, 'expires', res.json.expires_at, 'zip', fs.statSync(ZIP).size, 'bytes', 'digest', digest.slice(0, 12)); console.log('RAW', res.text.slice(0, 400)); })(); EOF node upload.js

y++) for (let x = x0; x < x0 + w; x++) v.push(lum(img, x, y)); const s = [...v].sort((a, b) => a - b); const bg = s[Math.floor(s.length * 0.5)], pk = s[Math.floor(s.length * 0.995)], thr = bg + (pk - bg) * 0.45;
